Method: 提出A-CRC-QA框架：(1) 将选择条件下的错误控制重构为线性期望约束；(2) 应用受置信风险控制启发的单调化经验风险校准程序；(3) 由于实例级损失相对于接受阈值一般是非单调的，框架针对渐近而非有限样本风险控制；(4) 模型无关，无需额外训练，可与不同不确定性估计器结合。

Result: 在CoQA（开放式问答）和MedMCQA（封闭式问答）上的实验表明，与未校准和基于置信边界的基线相比，A-CRC-QA在已接受答案可靠性与答案保留率之间取得了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: A-CRC-QA为LLM选择性问答提供了具有统计保证的后验校准方法，兼顾了可靠性和保留率，且具有广泛的适用性（模型无关、无需训练）。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate fluent but incorrect answers, making uncertainty quantification important for reliable question answering. However, heuristic uncertainty scores cannot perfectly distinguish correct predictions from incorrect ones, and directly applying a fixed uncertainty threshold provides no statistical control over the error rate among accepted answers. To address this limitation, we propose A-CRC-QA, a post-hoc calibration framework for uncertainty-aware selective question answering. The proposed method reformulates selection-conditioned error control as a linear expectation constraint and applies a monotonized empirical-risk calibration procedure inspired by conformal risk control. Since the resulting instance-wise loss is generally non-monotone with respect to the acceptance threshold, our framework targets asymptotic rather than finite-sample risk control. A-CRC-QA is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and can be combined with different uncertainty estimators. Experiments on CoQA and MedMCQA demonstrate its applicability to both open-ended and closed-ended question answering, achieving a favorable trade-off between accepted-answer reliability and answer retention compared with uncalibrated and confidence-bound-based baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System for Bangla Regional Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12018)
*Rakib Ullah,Ruhul Islam Rahul,Tanbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的跨方言神经机器翻译系统，覆盖孟加拉语的12种区域方言，无需通过标准中间语言转译。系统构建了迄今最大的孟加拉语多方言平行语料库（51,531句对），微调BanglaT5模型并采用DoRA技术，取得29.26 BLEU和57.26 chrF++的最优性能，超越NLLB-200和mBART-50，并部署为开源的INT8量化Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语拥有超过2.4亿使用者，存在显著的区域方言差异，在音韵、形态和词汇上与标准口语孟加拉语（SCB）差异巨大。现有NMT架构和LLM假设语言分布均匀，导致低资源方言翻译性能严重下降。

Method: 构建包含51,531个非空平行句对、覆盖12种方言的多方言平行语料库，其中包含2,500个专家验证的五种新方言的双向平行句对。在序列到序列架构下，采用权重分解低秩适配（DoRA）微调BanglaT5模型，实现多方向方言翻译。

Result: 微调的BanglaT5模型在12种孟加拉语方言翻译中达到最优性能（29.26 BLEU，57.26 chrF++），优于NLLB-200（615M）和mBART-50（611M），同时保持形态连贯性。INT8量化模型已部署为开源Web应用，数据集在Mendeley Data公开发布。

Conclusion: 本文证明了无需中间标准语枢纽、直接进行多方向方言翻译的可行性，建立了低资源方言适应的经验阈值，并通过开源部署促进边缘化方言社区的数字包容。

Abstract: Regional dialectal variation poses a fundamental challenge to natural language processing (NLP) in Bangla, where over 240 million speakers communicate across diverse regional variants that diverge significantly from Standard Colloquial Bangla (SCB) in phonology, morphology, and lexicon. Contemporary neural machine trans- lation (NMT) architectures and large language models (LLMs) predominantly as- sume a homogeneous language distribution, resulting in severe performance degra- dation when translating low-resource regional dialects. In this work, we present a unified Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System capable of multi-directional translation across 12 Bangla regional dialects without routing through an inter- mediary standard pivot. We compile the largest multi-dialect parallel corpus for Bangla to date, comprising 51,531 non-null parallel sentence pairs across 12 di- alects, incorporating 2,500 expert-verified, bidirectional parallel sentence pairs for five previously unaddressed dialects. Evaluating sequence-to-sequence architec- tures under Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA), our fine-tuned BanglaT5 model achieves state-of-the-art translation performance (29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++), outperforming NLLB-200 (615M) and mBART-50 (611M) while preserving morphological coherence. Furthermore, we conduct a systematic cross- dialectal transfer analysis and dataset scaling study, establishing empirical thresh- olds for low-resource dialect adaptation. Finally, we deploy the optimized INT8- quantized model as an open-access web application to promote digital inclusion for marginalized dialect communities. The complete dataset is publicly available at Mendeley Data (https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/v9cf66fk2t/2).

</details>


### [146] [Preference Tree Optimization: Enhancing Goal-Oriented Dialogue with Look-Ahead Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12062)
*Lior Baruch,Moshe Butman,Kfir Bar,Doron Friedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了偏好树优化（PTO）框架，通过偏好树与前瞻（Look-Ahead）方法生成偏好数据，结合直接偏好优化（DPO）迭代提升对话智能体的性能，在动机访谈（MI）领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发多轮、目标导向的对话系统仍具挑战，尤其是在数据稀缺的专业领域。现有方法难以在有限数据条件下有效提升智能体的决策与长期规划能力。

Method: 提出PTO框架：1）利用虚拟患者和oracle评估器模拟对话，通过"偏好树与前瞻"方法生成丰富的偏好数据集；2）结合DPO在迭代训练循环中增强智能体的决策能力；3）通过不同深度的look-ahead配置探索长期规划策略。

Result: 实验表明PTO框架在动机访谈领域显著提升对话智能体表现，在会话满意度和工作联盟等关键指标上持续优于基线；look-ahead模拟改善了长期规划，更深的look-ahead配置获得最稳定且高分的结果。

Conclusion: PTO框架有效解决了目标导向对话领域的数据稀缺问题，通过偏好树生成与前瞻模拟相结合的方式，推动了更加精细和有效的对话系统发展。

Abstract: Developing dialogue systems capable of engaging in multi-turn, goal-oriented conversations remains a significant challenge, especially in specialized domains with limited data. This research proposes a novel framework called Preference Tree Optimization (PTO), designed to iteratively improve agent models in such dialogue systems, by generating preference data using a method called Preference Tree with Look-Ahead. Focusing on Motivational Interviewing (MI) -- a counseling technique aimed at facilitating behavioral change -- we leverage virtual patients and an oracle evaluator to simulate conversations and generate rich preference datasets. By combining this method with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), we aim to enhance the agent's decision-making capabilities over iterative training cycles. The proposed framework addresses data scarcity and advances the development of more nuanced and effective dialogue systems in goal-oriented domains. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that the PTO framework enhances dialogue agents' performance in goal-oriented conversations within the domain of Motivational Interviewing (MI). Models trained with PTO consistently outperformed the baseline in key metrics such as session satisfaction and working alliance. Additionally, incorporating look-ahead simulations led to improved long-term planning and more effective conversational strategies, with deeper look-ahead configurations yielding the most stable and high-scoring results.

</details>


### [147] [Structuring the Space of Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12113)
*Agnese Daffara,Sebastian Padó,Tanise Ceron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文回顾了NLP领域中"视角"（perspective）相关的研究空间，提出了一组用于区分不同视角相关概念（如立场、情感、框架、论点等）的属性，并基于这些属性构建了一个将这些概念沿单一轴线性组织起来的层级结构，帮助研究者根据研究目标选择合适的操作化定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一事件可以从不同视角被报道，NLP多个子领域都在处理视角问题，但已有的操作性概念（立场、情感、框架、论点等）之间的关系尚不清楚，缺乏更深层次的理论理解来指导有效研究。

Method: 通过系统性回顾NLP中的视角研究空间，定义一组帮助区分视角相关概念的属性，基于这些属性提出一个将这些概念沿单一轴线性组织的概念层级结构。

Result: 提出了一个原则性的概念层级结构，该结构将视角相关概念沿单一轴线性排列，并证明该层级能帮助研究者导航该领域，选择与特定研究目标相一致的视角操作化方案。

Conclusion: 对视角相关概念更深入的理论理解可以赋能更有效的研究，所提出的概念层级为研究者在NLP中选择和操作化视角提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The same event can be reported from different perspectives depending on the experiences, background, and beliefs of the writer or speaker. A variety of NLP areas engage with perspectives, spanning from text analysis to algorithm optimization. A wide range of operative concepts (such as stances, sentiment, frames, and arguments) has been used to capture perspectives in texts, however the precise relationships among those concepts remain unclear. Arguably, a deeper theoretical understanding of these concepts would empower more effective research on perspectives. In this paper, we address this gap by reviewing the space of perspectives in NLP and defining a set of properties that help distinguishing perspective-related concepts. Our analysis leads us to posit a hierarchy which organizes these concepts linearly along a single axis. Finally, we show how this principled conceptual hierarchy can help researchers navigate the field and select operationalizations of perspective that align with their specific research objectives.

</details>


### [148] [QV-PIC: Query-Aware Visual Position-Independent Caching for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12121)
*Yilin Liu,Rui Meng,Wangze Ni,Jianxin Yan,Heng Cao,Libin Zheng,Peng Cheng,Jinfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QV-PIC是一个查询感知的双分辨率PIC复用框架，通过模型原生模板离线编译视觉缓存，并在线通过累计查询相关性分数在低分辨率全局上下文和高分辨率细粒度文本证据之间权衡，从而在不牺牲效率的前提下弥合渲染图像PIC与文本PIC之间的质量差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中重复预填充相同文本块造成冗余计算，现有PIC（位置无关缓存）通过复用KV对缓解，但文本token量大限制效率；将文本渲染为图像可压缩为更少视觉token，但渲染图像PIC比文本PIC质量下降更严重，主要源于跨独立缓存间的上下文不匹配以及视觉压缩导致细粒度文本证据丢失，现有修复方法只解决前者且引入在线计算。

Method: QV-PIC框架包含两个阶段：离线阶段在模型原生聊天模板前缀下编译视觉缓存，无需在线重计算即可提升PIC质量；在线阶段以低分辨率保留全局上下文，并在高分辨率预算内通过累计查询相关性分数恢复细粒度文本证据，保持视觉压缩的效率优势。

Result: 在六个任务上，QV-PIC相比原始渲染图像PIC平均F1提升21.6分，缩小了与文本PIC的差距并超越优化后的文本PIC 2.58个F1点，同时TTFT降低17.2%；相比全预填充，TTFT削减83.8%。

Conclusion: QV-PIC通过查询感知的双分辨率设计和模型原生模板引导，成功解决了渲染图像PIC的质量短板，在保持视觉压缩效率优势的同时实现了超越文本PIC的性能，为RAG系统中的PIC复用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) repeatedly prefills identical text chunks across queries, incurring redundant computations. Position-Independent Caching (PIC) mitigates it by reusing precomputed Key-Value (KV) across positions, but its efficiency is constrained by the large volume of text tokens. Rendering text chunks as images can compress the text into fewer visual tokens, but the rendered-image PIC suffers more severe quality degradation than the text PIC. This representation-specific gap primarily arises from contextual mismatches across independently compiled caches and the loss of fine-grained textual evidence during visual compression. Existing PIC repair methods mainly address the former through selective recomputation, but they incur online computation and cannot recover lost textual details. We propose QV-PIC, a query-aware dual-resolution PIC reuse framework guided by model-native templates. Offline, QV-PIC compiles visual caches under the model's native chat-template prefix, improving PIC quality without online recomputation. Online, it preserves global context with low resolution and restores fine-grained textual evidence within a high-resolution budget by cumulative query relevance scores, retaining the efficiency benefit of visual compression. Across six tasks, QV-PIC improves average F1 by 21.6 points over vanilla rendered-image PIC, closes the gap to vanilla text PIC, and surpasses optimized text PIC by 2.58 F1 while reducing TTFT by 17.2\%. Relative to full prefill, it cuts TTFT by 83.8%.

</details>


### [149] [SAG: SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation with Query-Time Dynamic Hyperedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12129)
*Yuchao Wu,Junqin Li,XingCheng Liang,Yongjie Chen,Yinghao Liang,Linyuan Mo,Guanxian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAG（SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation），一种结构化检索架构，不构建全局知识图谱，而是将文档组织为"事件-实体"索引，将每个块表示为语义完整的事件及其实体组成的潜在超边，保留n元关系。查询时以共享实体作为连接键动态构建查询范围的邻域，在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上均取得最佳检索和端到端QA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流稠密检索方法在处理结构化约束和多跳推理时存在固有限制；基于图的方法虽能解决此问题，但存在语义碎片化、维护成本高和增量更新复杂等缺陷。作者希望设计一种无需构建全局知识图谱的结构化检索架构。

Method: SAG将文档块表示为"事件-实体"对，形成保留n元关系的潜在超边而非分解为三元组。查询时将共享实体作为连接键关联相关块，动态生成查询范围的邻居事件集，同时保持每条证据都是原始块。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个基准上均取得最佳检索和端到端QA性能，且优势随推理链复杂度增加而扩大。在MuSiQue上Recall@5达到80.36%，超过最强基线11.52个百分点。

Conclusion: SAG为持续增长的组织知识提供了高效的知识基础设施，使LLM智能体能够检索并推理不断扩展的知识，避免全局知识图谱的维护负担。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective at giving LLMs access to external knowledge, mainstream dense-retrieval implementations remain inherently limited in handling structured constraints and multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based methods address this by constructing knowledge graphs offline, but they often fragment semantics, incur high maintenance, and complicate incremental updates. We propose SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), a structured retrieval architecture that organizes documents into an event-entity index without building a global knowledge graph. SAG represents each chunk as a semantically complete event paired with its entities, forming a latent hyperedge that preserves n-ary relations without decomposing them into triples. At query time, SAG treats shared entities as join keys to connect related chunks. This dynamically yields a query-scoped neighborhood of events, and yet every piece of evidence remains the original chunk throughout. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue show that SAG achieves the best retrieval and end-to-end QA performance on every benchmark, with gains that widen as reasoning-chain complexity increases. On MuSiQue, where multi-hop evidence chaining is most demanding, SAG reaches 80.36% Recall@5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 11.52 points. This work paves the way for knowledge infrastructure that enables LLM agents to retrieve and reason over continually growing organizational knowledge.

</details>


### [150] [A corpus-specific clinical RAG system matches or outperforms newer frontier LLMs on HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12138)
*Praveen Reddy,Charuta Mandke,Suvrankar Datta,Sarah Khan,Siddharth Reddy Anthireddy,Shitij Arora,Vishal Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了VITA系统——一个专为印度及中低收入国家（LMIC）环境构建的检索增强生成（RAG）临床系统。在HealthBench基准上，VITA以51.9%的得分超越多个前沿通用LLM（GPT-5.4、o4-mini等）排名第一；在更新模型和中性裁判的严格测试中，VITA与GPT-5.5统计上不分伯仲。结果表明领域特定RAG系统在临床基准上仍可与前沿LLM竞争，语料特异性是提升接地性（grounding）的设计变量，但以沟通流畅性为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用LLM在医疗基准上的对比测试多基于高收入国家开发的基准和少量系统，缺乏对LMIC环境和专业领域系统的系统评估。作者旨在验证专为资源受限环境构建的临床RAG系统是否仍具竞争力。

Method: 构建VITA，一个针对印度及LMIC环境的RAG系统，从疾病指南、印度耐药数据、国家处方集和资源受限护理方案等定制语料库中检索。在HealthBench（4023个问题，占80.5%）上用GPT-4.1裁判评分；另取500题子集，用更新一代模型和与测试系统无血缘关系的中立开源裁判（DeepSeek-V4-Pro）进行稳健性验证。

Result: VITA在HealthBench上以51.9%的得分排名第一，超过GPT-5.4（46.1%）、o4-mini（44.3%）、Gemini 3.1 Pro（42.6%）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（37.3%）。在500题子集上（更新模型+中立裁判），VITA与GPT-5.5统计上无显著差异；VITA在准确性、完整性和点数加权得分上仍占优，但沟通得分较低。

Conclusion: 领域专用的临床RAG系统在开放基准上仍与前沿通用LLM保持竞争力。语料特异性是提升接地性的关键设计变量，但会牺牲一定的沟通润色质量。该结果为LMIC临床AI系统设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to match or exceed specialized clinical AI tools on medical benchmarks, but such comparisons draw on a narrow set of systems and on benchmarks developed largely in high-income settings. We evaluate VITA, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system purpose-built for contextual knowledge retrieval in India and other low- and middle-income (LMIC) settings. VITA retrieves from a curated corpus of disease-specific guidelines, India-specific antimicrobial resistance data, national formulary constraints, and resource-limited care protocols; its architecture and corpus are proprietary, but the benchmark, the physician-written rubrics, and our full response and scoring outputs are public for independent verification. On 4,023 English-language HealthBench questions (80.5% of the benchmark), scored with a GPT-4.1 judge, VITA ranked first with 51.9% of possible rubric points, ahead of GPT-5.4 (46.1%), o4-mini (44.3%), Gemini 3.1 Pro (42.6%), and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (37.3%), and scored highest on 45.4% of questions. To test robustness to newer models and judge lineage, a 500-question subset was re-run against current-generation models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.5 Pro, Grok 4.3) and graded by a neutral open-weight judge (DeepSeek-V4-Pro) sharing no lineage with any system tested. Here the gap narrowed to parity: VITA and GPT-5.5 were statistically indistinguishable on mean per-question score, while VITA led on points-weighted score and won the most questions. VITA's advantages in accuracy and completeness persisted under the neutral judge; its communication scores were lower. These results indicate that a purpose-built clinical RAG system remains competitive with frontier LLMs on an open benchmark, consistent with corpus specificity as a design variable that improves grounding at some cost to communication polish.

</details>


### [151] [Massive Activations in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models: Pre-Attention Spikes and Inter-Spike Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12149)
*Zunhai Su,Bohan Sun,Xialie Zhuang,Shuibai Zhang,He Xiao,Jing Xiong,Hengyuan Zhang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tiantian Zhang,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了层交错混合线性注意力（HLA）大语言模型中的Massive activations（MAs），发现两种与架构对齐的形态：全注意力层前的尖峰（PAS）和贯穿线性注意力层的平台（ISP），并从机制、规模和门控角度全面分析了其形成与演变规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对Massive activations的研究多集中于纯全注意力LLM，而在混合线性注意力（HLA）架构中，MAs的形态与行为尚无人系统探索。理解MAs在HLA架构中的组织规律，对于揭示大规模预训练中的异常激活机制和设计更优的混合注意力架构至关重要。

Method: 对五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置和五个数据域中的HLA模型进行系统性MA分析；在1.2B至397B参数规模的多个开源混合模型上验证；并通过最多1.3B参数的受控预训练GDN混合模型，研究输出门控对MA形态的影响；最后通过系统性离群值分析提出MA的生命周期解释模型。

Result: 发现两种架构对齐的MA形态：全注意力层前尖峰（PAS）和跨越线性注意力层的尖峰间平台（ISP）。随着全注意力密度增加，PAS通过ISP逐步连接，最终恢复纯全注意力LLM的稳定MA形态。两种形态在训练早期即出现，且对输出门控呈现不对称响应：全注意力输出门控强烈衰减MA幅度但不消除层内组织，移除GDN门控仅带来适度放大。

Conclusion: MA的形成遵循统一的生命周期机制——由MA取消（cancellation）的时机制约：PAS对应局部化的写入-衰减-取消过程，ISP的持续存在则与延迟取消一致。该机制在极限情况下可统一解释从混合注意力到全注意力LLM的MA形态演变。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of Massive activations (MAs) in layer-interleaved HLA LLMs and uncover two architecture-aligned morphologies: MAs consistently spike immediately before full attention layers, forming pre-attention spikes (PAS), and can persist through intervening linear attention layers, giving rise to inter-spike plateaus (ISP). As full attention becomes denser, successive PAS become increasingly connected through ISP, ultimately recovering the stable MA morphology of full attention LLMs. We establish the recurrence of this organization across five linear attention architectures, six hybridization configurations, five data domains, and representative open-source hybrid models spanning 1.2B to 397B total parameters. Controlled pretraining of GDN-based hybrids at scales up to 1.3B shows that both morphologies emerge early and respond asymmetrically to output gating: full attention output gating strongly attenuates their absolute magnitudes without eliminating their layerwise organization, whereas removing GDN gates yields comparatively modest amplification. Mechanistically, our systematic-outlier analysis supports a shared lifecycle account governed by the timing of MA cancellation. PAS follows a localized write-sink-cancel process, while the extended persistence of ISP is consistent with delayed cancellation. At the full attention limit, this account recovers the stable MA morphology characteristic of full attention LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartluxLabs/Massive-Activations-HLA.

</details>


### [152] [Information Abundance Paradox: Long-Context Training Undermines Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12218)
*Arda Uzunoglu,Benjamin van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息丰裕悖论"（Information Abundance Paradox），挑战了"训练上下文越长越好"的隐含假设。研究发现，预训练和微调中过长的上下文窗口会导致模型从参数化内化转向上下文依赖，在达到中间最优值后性能反而下降。机制上，丰富的上下文信息会减少模型将知识编码进参数的激励，将梯度压力从前馈网络转移到注意力模块，从而降低模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型不断扩展上下文窗口以适应长文档、代码库和交互历史，这基于"更长上下文=更丰富证据=更好模型"的隐含假设。作者质疑这一假设，认为上下文窗口会影响模型的学习模式（参数内化vs.上下文化），过长上下文可能适得其反。

Method: 通过预训练和指令微调实验系统性地改变上下文窗口长度，评估语言建模、自然语言理解和闭卷MCQA性能；在SFT中操纵训练时上下文的相关性和测试时上下文的可用性；通过梯度分析和因果干预实验，追踪梯度压力在注意力模块与前馈网络之间的分配变化，揭示参数化知识与上下文依赖之间的权衡机制。

Result: 预训练中增加上下文窗口仅在中间最优值之前提升性能，之后持续下降；SFT中更多训练时的任务相关上下文能改善有支撑上下文的性能，但降低无上下文或误导性上下文时的鲁棒性；机制上，信息丰富的上下文将梯度压力从前馈网络（参数化知识）转移到注意力模块（上下文处理），因果干预证实这种转移增加了推理时对上下文的依赖。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持"信息丰裕悖论"：向接近无限的上下文扩展不仅仅是提供更多数据的问题，即使高质量长上下文数据充足，也会削弱模型的参数化知识编码能力和鲁棒性。这提示上下文窗口的设计需要平衡而非最大化。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained and deployed with long contexts that span documents, code repositories, and interaction histories. This scaling reflects the implicit assumption that training on longer contexts will only help the model by exposing it to richer evidence. We challenge this view by studying how the context window shapes a model's mode of learning, shifting it between parametric internalization and contextualization. We propose the Information Abundance Paradox, which hypothesizes that abundant relevant information in the training context can reduce the incentive to encode that information parametrically, thereby increasing reliance on context. In pretraining with long documents, increasing the context window improves language modeling, natural language understanding, and closed-book MCQA only up to an intermediate optimum, after which performance consistently declines. In supervised fine-tuning, more task-relevant train-time context improves performance with supporting context, but reduces robustness when context is absent or misleading at test time. Our analysis suggests that this behavior arises when longer context provides a lower complexity solution. Mechanistically, training with informative context shifts gradient pressure from feed-forward networks, often linked to parametric knowledge, toward attention modules, and causal interventions show that this shift increases reliance on context during inference. Overall, these findings support the Information Abundance Paradox and suggest that scaling toward near-infinite context is not simply a matter of supplying more data, even when high-quality long-context data is abundant.

</details>


### [153] [One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12253)
*Simon Yu,Nicholas Tomlin,Marwa Abdulhai,Ximing Lu,Derek Chong,Abe Hou,Dilara Soylu,Sergey Levine,Christopher D. Manning,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体强化学习中"模拟器坍缩"(simulator collapse)问题：由于模拟用户行为的LLM发生模式坍缩，导致策略过拟合于模拟器的单一主导模式，泛化能力差。作者提出两种互补解决方案——推理时的Verbalized Sampling（通过言语化响应分布采样拓宽行为）和训练时的Co-Training（对可训练模拟器群体联合优化），在三个多轮基准上分别将保留成功率提升至9%和14%，并通过人类实验验证了真实用户上的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类-AI交互的多智能体强化学习通常依赖单一大型语言模型模拟用户行为，但作者发现这种方法系统性泛化失败。其根源在于模拟器LLM发生模式坍缩，导致训练出的策略过度拟合窄策略并利用模拟器的优势模式，难以迁移到未见过的模拟器和真实用户。这一核心问题限制了多轮RL在真实世界部署中的有效性。

Method: 作者首先对模拟器坍缩进行了理论形式化，随后提出两种互补方案：(1) 推理时方案Verbalized Sampling——通过从言语化响应分布中进行采样来拓宽模拟器行为，减少模式坍缩；(2) 训练时方案Co-Training——联合优化策略与可训练模拟器群体，防止策略过拟合于任何单一模拟器的模式。同时发布了支持群体协同训练多智能体RL的开源框架SCOPE。

Result: 在Persuasion for Good、τ²-bench和CooperBench三个多轮基准上，Verbalized Sampling相比单一模拟器RL将保留成功率提升最高9%，Co-Training进一步将增益推至14%；人类研究显示在真实用户上获得类似增益。两种方法均保留了在单一模拟器RL下坍缩的策略多样性。

Conclusion: 训练环境的多样性（而非仅策略多样性）对多轮RL向真实世界部署的泛化至关重要。模拟器群体的多样性是解决模拟器坍缩、提升策略鲁棒性的关键，为多智能体RL与人类交互的研究提供了新的方向和实践框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [154] [A Cascaded Unsupervised-Supervised NLP Pipeline for Detecting Accusatory Language in Public Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12269)
*Bryan Torres,Daniel Riofrío,José Vega-Sánchez,Nathaly Orozco,Carla Parra,Karen Rosero,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合NLP框架，结合无监督聚类（GMM）和监督分类（随机森林），分析厄瓜多尔公共采购系统（SOCE）中投标前的参与者评论，以识别潜在的采购违规行为。实验表明，域训练Word2Vec嵌入配合GMM聚类和随机森林分类器在严重类别不平衡下仍能取得高精确率和召回率，无需大规模计算基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购涉及大量财政资源，需要持续监督，但利益相关者的评论和公开政府数据常被忽视，未能充分利用其揭示程序违规的潜力。现有研究缺乏利用文本数据（特别是参与者评论）进行采购风险识别的系统方法。

Method: 提出一个混合建模框架，在NLP流水线中集成无监督聚类和监督分类。使用Word2Vec、LLaMA和RoBERTa生成语义嵌入，然后用高斯混合模型（GMMs）进行无监督聚类以发现潜在模式，最后应用监督分类阶段识别指控性或举报风格评论。核心组合是域训练Word2Vec + GMM聚类 + 随机森林分类器。

Result: 实验结果表明，域训练Word2Vec嵌入与GMM聚类及随机森林分类器的组合在严重类别不平衡条件下仍能达到高精确率和召回率，证明了轻量级、域适应的NLP方法能有效支持风险识别。

Conclusion: 轻量级、域适应的NLP架构可以有效支持公共采购系统的风险识别并增强透明度，无需依赖大规模计算基础设施。

Abstract: Public procurement involves the allocation of substantial financial resources; therefore, continuous oversight through audits, controls, and monitoring mechanisms is essential. However, stakeholder comments and publicly available government data are often underutilized, despite their potential to reveal procedural irregularities. To address this gap, this paper analyzes metadata from Ecuador's Sistema Oficial de Contratación Pública (SOCE, Official Public Procurement System), with particular emphasis on participant comments generated during the pre-contractual phase. We propose a hybrid modeling framework that integrates unsupervised clustering and supervised classification within a natural language processing (NLP) pipeline to uncover latent patterns and detect potentially irregular procurement processes. Semantic embeddings are generated using Word2Vec, LLaMA, and RoBERTa, followed by Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) for unsupervised clustering. A supervised classification stage is then applied to identify accusatory or whistleblowing-style comments. Experimental results show that the combination of domain-trained Word2Vec embeddings, GMM-based clustering, and a Random Forest classifier achieves high precision and recall, even under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, domain-adapted NLP architectures can effectively support risk identification and enhance transparency in public procurement systems without requiring large-scale computational infrastructure.

</details>


### [155] [Structural Silence: When AI Infrastructure Fails Speakers of Underrepresented Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12278)
*Avijit Roy,Proma Roy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过孟加拉语这一案例，揭示了AI教育工具在欠资源社区中面临的结构性障碍——包括网络内容差距、训练数据赤字、分词惩罚和连接性排除四大互锁失败，并提出应将数据稀缺视为结构性壁垒、将离线优先设计视为公平导向的基础设施策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育工具被宣传为缩小欠资源社区教育差距的可扩展方案，但其底层基础设施（训练语料、分词方案、评估基准、部署架构）在模型训练之前就已系统性边缘化小众语言使用者。作者选择孟加拉语（全球使用人数最多的语言之一）这一典型案例，揭示这些被忽视的结构性不公。

Method: 本文通过孟加拉语的实证案例，从四个维度系统分析AI教育工具的结构性障碍：（1）网络内容占比差距；（2）训练语料token赤字；（3）音节文字分词惩罚导致的token膨胀；（4）城乡连接性差异。并基于这些分析提出离线优先作为公平导向策略的论点。

Result: 研究发现四大互锁失败：孟加拉语占全球人口近4%却仅占全球网络内容不到0.5%；英语与孟加拉语在主要多语言语料库中的训练token比为67:1；孟加拉语音节文字的分词惩罚加剧了数据赤字；农村个人互联网普及率36.5%远低于城市的71.4%。这些失败反映了长期资源分配决策和设计默认值中未将小众语言置于中心。

Conclusion: 数据集稀缺应被理解为结构性壁垒而非孤立的技术限制，离线优先设计应被视为促进公平的基础设施策略。作者最后为语言学和AI研究提出减少这些结构性不平等的未来方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence tools for education and language support are increasingly framed as scalable responses to access gaps in under-resourced communities. Yet the infrastructure underlying these tools, including training corpora, tokenization schemes, evaluation benchmarks, and deployment architectures, can systematically disadvantage speakers of underrepresented languages before a model is trained.
  This paper examines these structural barriers through Bengali, one of the world's most widely spoken languages, focusing on AI-assisted education in low-connectivity environments. We identify four interlocking failures: a severe web presence gap, with Bengali accounting for less than 0.5% of global web content despite representing nearly 4% of the global population; a 67:1 training-token deficit between English and Bengali in major multilingual corpora; a tokenization penalty associated with Bengali's alphasyllabary script that compounds the data deficit through higher token fertility; and connectivity exclusion, with individual internet penetration at 36.5% in rural areas compared with 71.4% in urban areas.
  These failures reflect longstanding resource-allocation decisions, institutional priorities, and design defaults that did not center underrepresented languages in mainstream AI development. We argue that dataset scarcity should be understood as a structural barrier rather than an isolated technical limitation, and that offline-first design should be treated as an equity-oriented infrastructure strategy. We conclude with directions for linguistics and AI research aimed at reducing these structural inequalities.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Spectral graph clustering with inhomogeneous latent geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11321)
*Konstantin Avrachenkov,Lucas S. Sibemberg,Alexander Van Werde*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究在存在潜在混淆几何结构时进行谱聚类的问题。作者提出一个块潜在空间模型（block latent-space model），证明社区可以从更深的特征向量中恢复出来，并开发了DBSPEC算法——一种基于密度的谱聚类方法，只需近似定位信息特征值且对特征值分离不佳具有鲁棒性。该方法能处理一般潜在几何结构，克服了先前工作局限于均匀环面模型的限制，且理论预测与真实世界实验观察高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在潜在几何结构（confounding latent geometry）的情况下，传统谱聚类的领先特征向量可能被这种潜在几何所主导，而非真实社区结构，导致无法正确恢复社区。先前的工作仅能处理同质环形模型（homogeneous toroidal models），缺乏对一般潜在几何结构的处理能力。

Method: 1) 提出块潜在空间模型来建模带有潜在几何结构的社区发现；2) 通过极限积分算子（limiting integral operator）分析邻接矩阵的谱性质；3) 基于该谱结构开发DBSPEC算法，一种密度驱动的谱聚类方法，仅需近似定位信息特征值，且对特征值分离不佳鲁棒。

Result: 证明了社区信息位于更深层的特征向量中而非领先特征向量中；DBSPEC算法能够处理一般潜在几何结构；理论对信息特征值位置的预测与真实世界实验数据观察一致。

Conclusion: 通过深入谱的属性分析，证明了即使领先特征向量被潜在几何主导，社区仍可从更深的特征向量中恢复；提出的DBSPEC算法具有广泛适用性，且理论预测具有实证支持。

Abstract: We study spectral clustering in the presence of a confounding latent geometry. The leading eigenvectors may then be dominated by the latent geometry rather than by the communities. Nevertheless, we show in a block latent-space model that communities can be recovered from eigenvectors deeper in the spectrum. We analyze the spectral properties of the adjacency matrix through a limiting integral operator and use its structure to develop DBSPEC, a density-based spectral clustering algorithm that requires only approximate localization of the informative eigenvalue and is robust to poor eigenvalue separation. Crucially, this approach handles general latent geometries, overcoming restrictions to homogeneous toroidal models in prior works. Our theoretical predictions for the location of the informative eigenvalue notably align with observations in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [157] [Exploring the Social Life of Data: Finding Data You Can Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11395)
*Penny R. Atkins,Manish Parashar*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据使用图（data-usage graphs）作为科学数据基础设施的新层次，通过连接数据集与出版物、人员、机构、主题、软件、模型、工作流等使用轨迹，形成类似社交信任网络的数据信任机制。作者在National Data Platform (NDP)中实现了原型服务以验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据日益丰富，找到数据已不再是难题，找到可信任的数据才是核心挑战。传统元数据不足以支撑研究者判断数据的适用性与可信度，需要新的基础设施来支持"适合用途"与"可信"的判断。

Method: 提出数据使用图的概念：将数据集与经由其产生和使用的出版物、人员、机构、主题、软件、模型、工作流和其他数据集相连接，揭示数据的"社会生活"，生成数据使用描述符以补充传统元数据，并结合生产质量、来源、治理、语义清晰度和社区验证共同评估信任。

Result: 在National Data Platform (NDP)中实现了原型"数据洞察发现服务"，展示了数据使用图的可行性和价值。

Conclusion: 核心主张是流行度本身不建立信任，但结合适当的上下文历史可以生长信任。使用证据必须与生产质量、来源、治理、语义清晰度和社区验证相结合，才能支持负责任的数据判断。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the scale and tempo of scientific inquiry. Models can now search, integrate, and reason over data far beyond data repositories familiar to any individual researcher. Yet this expansion creates a prior problem: before a model can produce a trustworthy scientific result, it must locate data that are appropriate for the question, sufficiently reliable for the intended analysis, and accompanied by enough context to support responsible interpretation. As data becomes increasingly abundant, the challenge of finding data has been overcome by the challenge of finding data that you can trust.
  This paper explores how the social and empirical evidence that accumulates when data are used in research can be used, analogous to social trust networks, to determine fit for purpose and trust. Specifically, the paper explores data-usage graphs as a new layer of scientific data infrastructure. A data-usage graph connects datasets to the publications, people, institutions, topics, software, models, workflows, and other datasets through which they are produced and used. These connections reveal the {\it social life of data:} who has relied on a source, for which questions, in what combinations, with which methods, and with what observable impact. They can turn scattered traces of practice into data-usage descriptors that complement conventional metadata and support judgments of trust and fitness for purpose. The central claim is not that popularity establishes trust, but that this can be grown with appropriate contextual history. Usage evidence must therefore be combined with production quality, provenance, governance, semantic clarity, and community validation. The feasibility and value of data usage graphs is demonstrated by implementing the prototype data insights discovery service within the National Data Platform (NDP).

</details>


### [158] [Twitter and disability activism: leadership and relevant topics in the online conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11923)
*Terese Mendiguren-Galdospin,Koldobika Meso-Ayerdi,Jesús Ángel Pérez-Dasilva,María Ganzabal-Learreta,Ainara Larrondo-Ureta,Simón Peña-Fernández*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文分析了Twitter上使用#disability标签（国际残疾人日前前后后）生成的超过18,000条推文，利用Gephi软件和Force Atlas 2算法构建提及网络图，研究围绕该话题生成的对话社区、主要议题及关键参与者。结论发现，尽管提及网络较为分散，但Twitter成为拉丁美洲环境中获得制度与政治支持的发声平台，相关组织（如ONCE基金会）以及女性活动家在其中占据显著主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上信息的传播与病毒化已从多角度（包括数字行动主义）被广泛研究，但残障相关行动主义在数字空间中的对话生成与网络结构尚未得到充分关注。本研究旨在填补这一空白，分析Twitter上残障话题对话的社区结构、主题和主要参与者。

Method: 以所有包含#disability标签的推文（国际残疾人日前前后后）为样本，收集超过18,000条推文及近乎等量的提及（mentions），将这些提及解释为加权图中的边，使用Gephi软件并应用Force Atlas 2暴力算法构建网络图，重点分析围绕该标签生成的对话社区、主要议题及突出参与者。

Result: 研究发现提及网络非常分散，但Twitter充当了残障相关发声的平台，这些发声在拉丁美洲环境中获得了一定的制度性和政治性支持；与残障事业相关的组织（如ONCE基金会）有所参与；尤为突出的是女性活动家在该对话中的明显主导地位。

Conclusion: 尽管#disability话题下的提及网络呈分散状态，Twitter依然是残障议题数字行动主义的活跃场域，其对话受到机构与政治力量的加持，且女性活动家在话题引领中发挥着核心作用。

Abstract: The dissemination and viralization of information on social media has been widely studied from various perspectives, including that of digital activism. On the other hand, disability-related activism has conquered the online environment, thus obtaining a reach that goes beyond the offline space and generating dialogue in the digital sphere. This article analyses the conversation generated on Twitter, taking as a sample all the tweets with the #disability hashtag before and after the International Day of Persons with Disabilities. More than 18,000 tweets, containing almost as many mentions, were analysed and interpreted as the weighted edges of a graph created using Gephi software and applying the Force Atlas 2 brute force algorithm. The focus was placed on the conversational communities generated around that hashtag, their main themes and the prominent participants in them. In conclusion, although the network of mentions is very dispersed, Twitter is the setting for assertions that receive certain institutional and political support in the Latin American environment, for organisations related to the cause (such as the ONCE Foundation) and, above all, the clear predominance of female activists.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [MergirafSemi: A Language-Agnostic Semistructured Merge Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11345)
*Pedro Lopes,Paulo Borba,Paola Accioly,Guilherme Cavalcanti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MergirafSemi，一种语言无关的半结构化合并工具，通过轻量级具体语法树引导合并决策，在保留语言灵活性和效率的同时，减少虚假冲突并维持竞争性的合并准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合并工具多为基于行的非结构化比较，易产生虚假冲突或遗漏真实冲突；而完全结构化的合并工具计算开销高，语言特定的工具则开发维护成本大，亟需一种在准确性、效率和通用性之间取得平衡的解决方案。

Method: 提出语言无关的半结构化合并工具MergirafSemi，利用轻量级具体语法树(Concrete Syntax Trees)捕获结构信息，无需完整结构建模或语言特定实现，并在程序特定区域（如Java方法体）内应用基于行的合并策略。

Result: 通过跨多语言的真实合并场景实证研究，发现增加结构粒度虽改善自动冲突解决但可能更激进地合并从而漏掉真实冲突；MergirafSemi相比非结构化工具显著减少虚假冲突，相比半结构化语言特定工具在效果相当的同时具有更稳健的执行行为和显著更低的运行时开销。

Conclusion: MergirafSemi实现了结构感知合并能力与运行效率、语言通用性之间的良好平衡，在多数常见场景中优于现有工具，为合并工具设计提供了中间路线的有效验证。

Abstract: Developers frequently face merge conflicts when integrating concurrent changes. Most merge tools rely on unstructured, line-based comparisons, often producing spurious conflicts and missing actual conflicts. To address these limitations, structure-aware merge tools have been proposed, which leverage syntactic representations to improve merge accuracy. However, fully structured tools may incur higher computational cost, and language-specific tools require significant development and maintenance effort. To balance these trade-offs, we propose MergirafSemi, a language-agnostic semistructured merge tool that captures structural information without requiring full structural modeling or language-specific implementations, and applies line-based merging within specific program regions, such as method bodies in Java. Our tool leverages lightweight Concrete Syntax Trees to guide merging decisions while preserving flexibility and efficiency across languages. We evaluate our tool through an empirical study on real-world merge scenarios across multiple programming languages, comparing it with unstructured, semistructured, and structured tools. Our results show that increasing structural granularity improves automatic conflict resolution but can also lead to more aggressive merge decisions, increasing the number of missed actual conflicts. In contrast, MergirafSemi achieves a more balanced trade-off, reducing spurious conflicts while maintaining competitive accuracy and better runtime performance in most common scenarios. Compared to an unstructured tool, it substantially reduces spurious conflicts, and when compared to a semistructured language-specific tool, it achieves comparable effectiveness while exhibiting more robust execution behavior and significantly lower runtime overhead.

</details>


### [160] [The Devil Is in the Interface: Evaluating How Tool Architecture Shapes Coding Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11386)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hamidreza Saghir,Qianhui Wu,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Tong Wang,Kiran Lakkaraju,Kexin Pei,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统研究了编码智能体的"工具架构"设计维度——即工具如何被组织和暴露给模型。在仓库级问题修复任务上，对比六种工具架构（保持底层信息和动作相似），涉及三个角色共11,700条轨迹。发现即使工具能力相似，工具架构也会显著改变智能体行为：结构化低级接口提升一致性达4.7倍；自然语言搜索扩大仓库探索并增加相关文件访问超11%；Python CodeAct风格接口以41.6%更少的步骤和56.3%更低的token消耗达到同等任务性能。而轻量级文本认知脚手架工具（如记录中间推理）对行为影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于智能体工具的研究主要聚焦于扩展智能体能做什么（能力维度），但较少系统性地关注这些能力如何被组织和暴露给模型（工具架构维度）。作者认为这是一个被忽视但关键的设计维度，因为它决定了智能体如何获取信息和在环境中采取行动。

Method: 通过受控实验在仓库级问题修复任务上进行比较研究，设计六种工具架构，在保持底层信息和动作相似的情况下，仅改变工具的组织和暴露方式。涉及三个不同智能体角色，共分析11,700条轨迹数据。

Result: 1) 与仅提供bash工具的基线架构相比，更结构化的低级接口可将重复尝试的一致性提升高达4.7倍；2) 自然语言搜索将仓库探索范围扩大，相关文件访问量增加超11%；3) Python CodeAct风格接口以41.6%更少的步骤和56.3%更低的token消耗达到相同任务性能；4) 轻量级文本认知脚手架工具（如记录中间推理）对智能体行为影响有限。

Conclusion: 工具架构是影响编码智能体行为的重要设计维度，即使工具提供的能力相同，组织与暴露方式的不同也会带来显著的行为差异。研究为智能体工具设计提供了实证指导，表明结构化接口和高效交互方式（如CodeAct）比轻量级文本辅助工具更有价值。

Abstract: As large language models continue to improve, agentic systems are becoming increasingly important, and tools are a key design dimension because they determine how agents access information and take action in their environments. Prior work on agent tooling has primarily focused on expanding what agents can do, but has paid less systematic attention to how those capabilities are organized and exposed to the model. We refer to this latter design dimension as tool architecture. We study tool architecture in coding agents through controlled experiments on repository-level issue fixing, comparing six tool architectures that hold the underlying information and actions similar while varying how they are organized and exposed to the model, across three actors and a total of 11,700 trajectories. Our experiments show that, even when tools provide similar capabilities, tool architecture changes agent behavior: Compared to a basic architecture where the agent has only the bash tool, more structured low-level interfaces improve consistency across repeated attempts by up to 4.7 $\times$; natural-language search broadens repository exploration and increases access to relevant files by more than 11%; and Python CodeAct-style interfaces achieve similar task performance with 41.6% fewer steps and 56.3% lower token usage. By contrast, lightweight text-based cognitive-scaffolding tools, such as tools that let the agent record intermediate reasoning, have limited effect on actor behavior.

</details>


### [161] [GraphAlignCoder: Aligning Program and Proof Graphs for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11394)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GraphAlignCoder是一个训练框架，通过构建实现图和形式化证明流图，将显式的正确性结构（包括程序区域为何正确的图描述）转移到代码生成中，在多个基准上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的执行反馈训练方法只能判断代码是否失败，无法提供关于正确解决方案应如何组织的充分监督信号，限制了代码LLM对隐藏语义约束的理解。

Method: 1) 构建捕获程序区域间控制和依赖关系的实现图；2) 通过约束Lean管道生成证明迹并提取形式化证明流图；3) 先让模型学习可执行代码及图描述的"为何正确"信息，再将此知识整合到代码生成中。

Result: 在所有基准上一致优于基座模型、纯代码SFT和CodeRL。相比CodeRL，LiveCodeBench v6求解数从38增至50（+31.6%），BigCodeBench Hard从16增至23（+43.8%），BigCodeBench Full从359提升至363。

Conclusion: 验证图注入提供了初始推理增益，而验证到代码的整合对于跨基准的稳健迁移至关重要；显式正确性结构转移是提升代码生成能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically plausible programs that nevertheless violate hidden semantic constraints. Existing execution-feedback training methods identify whether a completed program fails, but provide limited supervision about how a correct solution should be organized. We introduce GraphAlignCoder, a training framework that transfers explicit correctness structure into code generation.
  GraphAlignCoder constructs an implementation graph that captures control and dependence among program regions. In parallel, a constrained Lean pipeline produces proof traces, from which we extract a formal proof-flow graph. The model first learns executable code together with graph-derived descriptions of why individual program regions are correct, and then consolidates this knowledge into code generation. GraphAlignCoder consistently outperforms the base model, code-only SFT, and CodeRL across all benchmarks. Compared with CodeRL, it increases the solved count from 38 to 50 on LiveCodeBench v6 and from 16 to 23 on BigCodeBench Hard, corresponding to relative gains of 31.6% and 43.8%, while also improving BigCodeBench Full from 359 to 363 tasks. The ablation study further shows that verification-graph injection produces the initial reasoning gain, while verification to code consolidation is essential for robust cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [162] [Simplifying Requirements Engineering in the Context of the LGPD: An LLM-Based Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11454)
*Cinara Gomes de Melo Carneiro,Renato de Freitas Bulcão Neto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）是否能在巴西通用数据保护法（LGPD）框架下简化需求工程（RE），通过利用现行立法自动生成用户故事和验收测试场景，评估结果显示高性能，证实了LLMs从软件初始阶段确保法规合规的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合规隐私法规对需求工程构成复杂挑战：将法律规范转化为软件需求既困难又耗时，需要探索自动化方法以简化该过程。

Method: 提出一种利用大型语言模型（LLMs）的方法，基于巴西通用数据保护法（LGPD）的现行立法，自动生成用户故事（User Stories）和验收测试场景（Acceptance Test Scenarios）。

Result: 评估结果显示该方法具有高性能，证实了LLMs在软件初始阶段确保法规合规方面的潜力。

Conclusion: LLMs能够有效简化需求工程过程中的法规合规工作，有望在软件开发生命周期早期实现隐私法规的自动对齐。

Abstract: Compliance with privacy legislation poses a complex challenge to Requirements Engineering (RE): translating legal norms into software requirements. In this context, this study investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can simplify RE within the framework of the Brazilian General Data Protection Law (LGPD). The proposed approach utilizes current legislation to automatically generate User Stories and Acceptance Test Scenarios. The evaluation results demonstrated high performance, confirming the potential of LLMs to ensure regulatory compliance from the software's inception.

</details>


### [163] [Do Influence Tactics Matter? Investigating Prompt Framing Effects in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11513)
*Alex Deaconu,Anubhav Gupta,Manaal Basha,Nicholas Haydu,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模实证探讨了心理学启发的说服性提示框架（基于Yukl & Falbe的八种影响策略）对LLM代码生成任务的影响，发现某些策略（尤其强调紧迫性的框架）会降低代码的正确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于软件工程任务，提示词措辞已知会影响模型性能，但心理学启发的说服性提示框架（人类用于说服他人的沟通策略）对LLM编码行为的影响此前尚未被探索。

Method: 将Yukl & Falbe的八种影响策略（如理性说服、奉承、交换等）操作化为可复现的提示模板，在五个开源LLM上使用LiveCodeBench和SWE-bench Verified两个基准进行评估，从功能正确性、质量、可维护性和安全性四个维度衡量代码输出。

Result: 某些影响策略诱导的提示框架（特别是强调紧迫性的框架）与降低的正确性和安全性相关，这是首个大规模关于软件工程任务中影响诱导提示框架的实证研究。

Conclusion: 研究为语言线索如何塑造LLM输出提供了见解，并为设计透明、可解释的人机交互代码生成提供了实用建议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows, helping developers write, debug, test, and maintain code. While prompt wording and structure are known to influence model performance, the impact of psychologically inspired prompt framings remains unexplored. This study investigates whether different psychology-based communication strategies that humans use to persuade or motivate others can lead to more effective prompt framing, which may, in turn, affect LLM behaviour in coding tasks. Drawing on Yukl & Falbe's well-known taxonomy, we operationalized eight influence tactics (like rational persuasion, ingratiation, and exchange) into reproducible prompt templates. These prompt templates were evaluated across five leading open-weight LLMs using two widely adopted benchmarks: LiveCodeBench and SWE-bench Verified. We assessed the resulting code output on four key software quality dimensions: functional correctness, quality, maintainability, and security. Our results show that certain influence-induced prompt framings, particularly those emphasizing urgency, were associated with reduced correctness and security. This work presents the first large-scale empirical study of influence-induced prompt framing in software engineering tasks, offering insights into how linguistic cues may shape LLM outputs. We conclude with practical insights for designing transparent and interpretable human-AI interactions in code generation.

</details>


### [164] [Harnessing LLMs for Document-Guided Fuzzing of Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11744)
*Bin Duan,Tarek Mahmud,Meiru Che,Yan Yan,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VistaFuzz，一种文档引导的模糊测试技术，利用本地开源的LLM从API文档中提取参数规格说明，生成同时满足参数约束和参数间依赖关系的输入，用于测试Python库的API。在12个Python库的7,718个API上评估，发现40.1%的API存在参数间依赖关系，并报告了74个问题，其中43个已获开发者确认，29个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python库在深度学习、科学计算等领域至关重要，其可靠性直接影响下游应用。现有API测试方法要么将参数约束隐含在生成程序中，要么依赖库特定的解析规则，无法有效处理参数间的依赖关系。

Method: VistaFuzz使用本地部署的开源LLM从API文档中提取参数规格说明，并生成满足单参数约束和参数间依赖关系的输入。方法包括三个关键环节：文档解析、参数规格提取和输入生成。

Result: 在12个Python库的7,718个API上评估，发现40.1%的被测API存在参数间依赖关系；禁用依赖解析后，这些API的有效生成率从95%以上降至31.6%-52.8%。VistaFuzz共报告74个问题，其中43个获开发者确认，29个已修复。

Conclusion: VistaFuzz通过LLM从文档中提取参数规格并解析参数间依赖关系，显著提升了API测试输入的有效生成率，证明了文档引导结合LLM的模糊测试方法在Python库测试中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Python libraries underpin deep learning, scientific computing, data analysis, and computer vision, making their reliability critical to downstream applications. Testing their APIs requires inputs that satisfy both per-parameter constraints and dependencies among parameters. Existing approaches either leave such constraints implicit in generated programs or rely on library-specific parsing rules. This paper introduces VistaFuzz, a document-guided fuzzing technique that uses a locally served open-sourced LLM to extract parameter specifications from API documents and generate inputs that satisfy both parameter constraints and inter-parameter dependencies. We evaluate VistaFuzz on 7,718 APIs across twelve Python libraries. Inter-parameter relationships occur in 40.1\% of tested APIs, and disabling their resolution reduces the valid generation rate on those APIs from above 95\% to 31.6\%--52.8\%. VistaFuzz reports 74 issues, of which 43 have been confirmed by developers and 29 have been fixed.

</details>


### [165] [Instruction Alignment for Binary Code Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11766)
*Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在二进制代码表示学习中显式加入指令级对齐作为辅助训练目标，利用编译器调试信息提供的细粒度监督信号，提升函数级嵌入的质量和判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制代码表示学习方法主要学习函数级嵌入，捕捉粗粒度的语义关系，忽略了细粒度的指令级对应关系，从而丢失了编译器调试信息中可用的宝贵监督信号，限制了表示学习的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 通过初步研究发现，针对函数级二进制代码相似性微调的模型比预训练模型具有明显更好的指令对齐能力，表明指令对齐与函数级嵌入质量强相关。基于此观察，设计了一种训练方法，将指令对齐作为辅助训练目标显式融入学习过程中。

Result: 实验表明，指令对齐训练提升了检索准确率，并为模型的相似性判断提供了更具判别力的信号。

Conclusion: 指令级对齐知识可以作为有效的辅助监督信号，进一步提升二进制代码表示学习的质量和可解释性。

Abstract: Binary code representation learning is a fundamental problem in software security and reverse engineering. Existing methods mainly learn function-level embeddings that capture coarse-grained semantic relationships between binary functions, but they largely ignore fine-grained instruction-level correspondences. This limitation misses valuable supervision signals available from compiler debug information, which can support the learning of more accurate and interpretable binary code representations.
  We propose to leverage instruction alignment knowledge to further improve binary code representation learning. Our preliminary study reveals that models finetuned for function-level binary code similarity exhibit substantially better instruction alignment than their pre-trained model, suggesting a strong correlation between instruction alignment and function-level embedding quality. Motivated by this observation, we design a training approach that explicitly incorporates instruction alignment as an auxiliary training objective. Our experiments show that instruction alignment training improves retrieval accuracy and provides more discriminative signal for the model's similarity judgments.

</details>


### [166] [Testing Deep Learning Library APIs via Cross-Framework Differential Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11886)
*Bin Duan,Ruican Dong,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Xamt是一种跨框架的差分模糊测试方法，用于深度学习库API测试。它通过显式API别名和参数角色规范化构建跨七个库的执行验证组，并使用方差引导的差分模糊测试发现缺陷。共构建676组、匹配2563个API，识别72个不一致案例，25个已被开发者确认，23个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习库的API级测试技术依赖库内属性或CPU-GPU差分预言机，可能漏掉在跨硬件后端表现一致的缺陷。需要一种跨框架的差分测试方法来发现这些缺陷。

Method: Xamt方法包括：(1) 利用显式API别名和参数角色归一化构建候选API对应关系；(2) 通过成对执行和组级行为检查在普通输入上验证执行有效性；(3) 使用方差引导的差分模糊测试，覆盖普通、边界和非有限输入；(4) 通过崩溃和不一致性预言机标记异常。

Result: 在七个库上构建676个执行验证组（含2563个匹配API），识别72个可复现的差异案例（4个崩溃，68个输出不一致）。其中25个已获开发者确认，23个已修复。

Conclusion: 跨框架差分模糊测试是发现深度学习库API缺陷的有效方法，Xamt证明了其在规模化发现和确认真实缺陷方面的能力。

Abstract: Deep learning libraries underpin many safety- and reliability-critical applications, yet existing API-level testing techniques often rely on intra-library properties or CPU--GPU differential oracles and may miss defects that behave consistently across hardware backends. We present Xamt, a cross-framework differential fuzzing approach for deep learning library APIs. Xamt constructs and tests execution-validated groups of APIs intended to implement equivalent operations across seven libraries. It uses explicit API aliases and parameter-role normalization to construct candidate correspondences and validates them through pairwise execution and a group-level behavioral check on canonical ordinary inputs. The resulting groups are explored using variance-guided differential fuzzing with ordinary, boundary, and non-finite inputs. Crash and inconsistency oracles flag executions exhibiting abnormal termination or inconsistent outputs for subsequent reproduction and analysis. Across the seven libraries, Xamt constructs 676 execution-validated groups containing 2,563 matched APIs. Among these, Xamt identifies 72 independently reproduced discrepancy cases, including 4 crash cases and 68 output inconsistencies. Among the 72 developer reports, 25 have been confirmed, including 23 that have been fixed.

</details>


### [167] [Developing LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering: A Mixed-Method Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11965)
*Mariama Celi Serafim De Oliveira,Motunrayo Osatohanmen Ibiyo,Marco Gianrusso,Claudio Di Sipio,Davide Di Ruscio,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对软件工程中实现多智能体系统（MAS）的现有工具和框架进行了全面综述，通过定量分析评估了最相关的开源MAS框架的文档、特性和能力，并通过实现README.MD文件摘要这一共同用例对部分框架进行了定性评估。结果表明所选框架对MAS基本组件覆盖良好，但代理遥测等高级功能仍缺失；在摘要任务上各框架的ROUGE分数无显著差异。最后提供了经验教训和挑战，帮助研究人员和从业者根据需求选择合适的MAS框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（特别是LLM）已深刻改变软件开发过程，应用已从单一独立LLM辅助开发者转向多智能体系统（MAS）编排多个LLM代理协作完成共同目标。然而，使用MAS给开发者带来了一系列挑战，包括需要谨慎选择合适技术、设计协调规则以及为参与的代理设定特定角色。因此，需要对现有MAS工具和框架进行全面概述，以帮助开发者做出合理选择。

Method: 采用混合方法：(1) 定量分析：对最相关的开源MAS框架进行系统评估，从开发者视角考察其文档、特性和能力；(2) 定性评估：选取部分框架实现一个共同用例（README.MD文件摘要），利用ROUGE分数进行实证比较。最后基于分析结果总结经验教训和挑战。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 所选框架对MAS的基本组件提供了良好覆盖，但代理遥测（telemetry）等高级功能仍缺失；(2) 在README.MD摘要任务上，不同框架之间的ROUGE分数没有显著差异；(3) 提供了可帮助研究人员和从业者按需选择MAS框架的经验教训和挑战清单。

Conclusion: 本文提供了软件工程领域MAS框架的全面概述，通过定量与定性相结合的方法揭示了当前框架的能力覆盖情况与性能表现。研究结论强调框架在基础组件上已较成熟，但高级功能仍有待完善，且不同框架在典型任务上的表现并无显著差异，因此选择框架时更需关注非功能性需求和具体应用场景。

Abstract: The proliferation of Generative Artificial Intelligence (Gen AI) powered by large language models (LLMs) has transformed the software development process, introducing new paradigms for code generation, debugging, testing, and maintenance. While early applications focused on leveraging single, independent LLMs to assist developers with isolated tasks, recent advances have shifted toward multi-agent systems (MAS) that orchestrate multiple LLM-based agents working collaboratively toward common objectives. Despite their promising potential, using MAS encompasses a set of challenges for developers who have to carefully select the right technology, devise proper coordination rules, and design specific roles for the involved agents. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive overview of the existing tools and frameworks for implementing MAS in software engineering. First, we conducted a quantitative analysis of the most relevant open source MAS frameworks by evaluating their documentation, features, and capabilities from the developers' perspective. Second, we performed a qualitative evaluation of a subset of the selected frameworks by implementing a common use case: the summarization of README.MD files. The findings show that the selected frameworks provide a good coverage of fundamental components of MAS, though advanced features such as telemetry of agents are still missing. In addition, the empirical evaluation shows that there is no significant difference in terms of ROUGE scores considering the summarization task. Finally, we provide a set of lessons learned and challenges that can help researchers and practitioners to select a suitable MAS framework according to their needs.

</details>


### [168] [RealisticTritonBench: A Benchmark for Triton-Kernel Generation in Real-World AI Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12004)
*Jinjun Huang,Zhongzhen Wen,Tongtong Xu,Meng Yan,Xin Xia,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RealisticTritonBench，首个从真实AI框架的pull request中提取Triton内核生成任务的基准，通过端到端测试而非单内核性能评估LLM生成的Triton内核，结果发现现有LLM在真实世界的Triton内核生成任务上仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM生成Triton内核的基准存在三个关键局限：(1)仅局限于PyTorch到Triton的翻译任务，无法反映真实世界中Triton任务的多样性与复杂性；(2)只评估单个内核性能而非端到端性能（后者才是AI框架实际部署的核心标准）；(3)依赖人工编写的评估脚本，可能存在缺陷被模型利用以绕过正确性检查获得虚高分数。

Method: RealisticTritonBench从主流开源AI框架中系统地提取修改Triton内核的pull request，将其转化为带有具体工程背景的生成任务。每个任务以自然语言需求为输入，要求生成对应的Triton内核实现，并提供完整可复现的评估环境。与先前仅关注孤立内核性能的基准不同，该基准将生成的内核集成回原始框架，使用端到端测试进行评估。

Result: 在RealisticTritonBench上评估了领先的LLM，发现它们在真实世界的Triton内核生成任务上仍然表现困难。

Conclusion: 真实世界的Triton内核生成任务对当前LLM仍具挑战性，需要更贴近生产环境的基准来推动该领域发展。

Abstract: In modern AI frameworks, GPU kernels are key to overall system performance. Combining usability, portability, and near-handwritten CUDA performance, Triton is widely adopted for implementing GPU kernels. Recent advances show the potential of large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate Triton kernels, reducing the manual effort required from expert kernel developers. Several benchmarks evaluate LLM-generated Triton kernels. However, they suffer from three key limitations: (1) they restrict tasks to PyTorch-to-Triton translation, failing to reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world Triton tasks; (2) they evaluate only individual-kernel performance rather than end-to-end performance, the core criterion for real-world deployment in AI frameworks; and (3) they rely on manually written evaluation scripts for individual kernels, which may contain flaws that models can exploit to bypass correctness checks and obtain inflated scores. To address these limitations, we introduce RealisticTritonBench, the first benchmark to derive Triton kernel generation tasks from real-world pull requests in popular AI frameworks, enabling realistic, production-like evaluation. RealisticTritonBench systematically extracts PRs that modify Triton kernels from popular open-source AI frameworks and transforms them into generation tasks with concrete engineering contexts. Each task takes a natural language requirement as input and requires a corresponding Triton kernel implementation, with a complete and reproducible evaluation environment. Unlike prior benchmarks focused on isolated kernel performance, RealisticTritonBench integrates generated kernels into their original frameworks and evaluates them using end-to-end tests, enabling a more faithful assessment. We evaluate leading LLMs on RealisticTritonBench and find that they still struggle with real-world Triton kernel generation tasks.

</details>


### [169] [From Safety Documentation to Safety Knowledge Support: An Evidence-Grounded LLM Framework for Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12025)
*Tuhinangshu Gangopadhyay,Rasmus Adler,Peter Liggesmeyer,Jan Reich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个证据支撑的框架（evidence-grounded framework），将LLM应用于医疗器械安全工程中的知识支持任务。框架通过连接设备工件、受控知识存储与检索、方法特定的候选安全项生成、批判性与不确定性检查以及记录在案的人工专家审查，来辅助而非替代专家决策。核心论点是从“安全文本生成”转向“带来源链接的安全知识支持”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗器械开发需要符合ISO 14971和IEC 62304的风险管理证据，这些证据需在需求、设计、变更、验证、投诉和售后数据间保持一致。现有工作成本高且依赖稀缺专家；而当前LLM安全工程研究多针对孤立方法、依赖通用提示词或公开示例，对来源链接、可追溯性、不确定性处理、生命周期更新和专家审查记录支持不足，限制了其在受监管环境中的应用。

Method: 提出一个证据支撑框架，包含五个关键组成部分：(1) 连接设备工件；(2) 受控知识存储与检索；(3) 方法特定的候选安全项生成；(4) 批判性与不确定性检查；(5) 记录在案的人工专家审查。框架负责准备、链接、检查和更新候选安全工件以供专家决策，但不做出设备是否安全的判断，也不提供监管批准。同时概述了使用非公开或新建医疗器械案例研究及专家参考分析的评估策略。

Result: 论文尚未给出实证结果，而是提出了框架和评估策略。评估维度包括：覆盖率、正确性、相关性、可追溯性、重复率、无依据声明以及审查工作量。

Conclusion: 该论文认为医疗器械安全工程的核心研究问题不是安全文本生成，而是来源链接的安全知识支持。所提出的框架旨在通过受控的、带来源的证据流来辅助专家的安全决策，并以明确界定的评估策略为其后续实证验证提供了路径。

Abstract: Medical devices are becoming more software-intensive, connected, and AI-enabled. Their development requires risk-management evidence aligned with ISO 14971 and, for software, IEC 62304. This evidence must be kept consistent across requirements, design decisions, software changes, verification results, complaints, and post-market data. These tasks are costly and depend on scarce safety and domain experts.
  Large language models (LLMs) may reduce parts of this effort because medical-device safety work is highly document-based. However, current LLM-based safety-engineering studies often address isolated methods, rely on generic prompting or public examples, and provide limited support for source links, traceability, uncertainty handling, lifecycle updates, and recorded expert review. This limits their use in regulated medical-device development.
  This paper argues that the central research problem is not safety-text generation, but source-linked safety-knowledge support. We propose an evidence-grounded framework that connects device artifacts, controlled knowledge storage and retrieval, method-specific generation of candidate safety items, critique and uncertainty checks, and recorded expert review. The framework prepares, links, checks, and updates candidate safety artifacts for expert decision-making. It does not decide whether a device is safe and does not provide regulatory approval. We also outline an evaluation strategy using non-public or newly built medical-device case studies and expert reference analyses to assess coverage, correctness, relevance, traceability, duplicate rate, unsupported claims, and review effort.

</details>


### [170] [ADEPT: A Unified Framework for Deep Learning Test Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12144)
*Yidi Kao,Shawn Burnham,Tommi Rose Fahy,Ali Ghanbari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ADEPT框架，一个集成了多种深度学习测试充分性度量的统一框架。它通过一致的执行工作流、基于模板的度量接口、YAML配置管理、预处理缓存复用和结构化结果报告，解决了现有充分性度量工具难以复现、比较和采用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年提出了许多深度学习测试充分性度量指标，但这些指标通常以独立的研究原型形式发布，具有截然不同的安装要求、预处理需求、执行工作流和配置机制，导致它们难以复现、比较和在实际研究与部署中采用。

Method: ADEPT框架集成了多种代表性充分性技术（如神经元覆盖度、惊喜充分性、输入分布覆盖、边界覆盖、源级和模型级变异分数），提供：（1）基于模板的度量接口和明确的扩展点；（2）YAML配置管理；（3）预处理缓存复用；（4）结构化结果报告。

Result: ADEPT提供了一个统一执行工作流的框架，使研究人员和从业者能够在无需花费数天或数周实现缺失工具或配置不同研究原型的情况下，复现和应用充分性度量指标。演示视频可在指定网址获取。

Conclusion: ADEPT旨在为研究和开发工作流中的充分性度量应用提供易用性和一致性，降低了度量工具集成的门槛，促进充分性度量在实际应用中的采用。

Abstract: Over the past decade, many test adequacy metrics have been proposed for deep learning that characterize test dataset adequacy from different perspectives, e.g., neuron activation behavior, latent feature coverage, decision-boundary exploration, etc. However, these metrics are typically released as independent research prototypes with substantially different installation and preprocessing requirements, execution workflows, and configuration mechanisms. These complications make them quite difficult to reproduce, compare, and adopt in research work and practical deployment alike. In this paper, we present the engineering details of ADEPT, a framework that integrates representative adequacy techniques, including neuron-coverage-based metrics, surprise adequacy, input distribution coverage, boundary coverage, and source- and model-level mutation score, under a consistent execution workflow. ADEPT provides a template-based metric interface with well-defined extension points for integrating new adequacy metrics. Furthermore, it provides YAML-based configuration management, preprocessing-cache reuse, and structured result reporting, making it easy to use in any research and development workflows. ADEPT is designed for researchers and practitioners who wish to reproduce and apply adequacy metrics without spending days or weeks implementing missing tooling or configuring disparate research prototypes. A demo video is available at https://aub.ie/ADEPT_video.

</details>


### [171] [Towards Automated Domain Model Extraction from Source Code using Heuristics and Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12228)
*Alessandra Mancas,Mounir Ammam,Hyacinth Ali,Kevin Delcourt,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种使用轻量级、可本地部署的LLM从源代码中自动提取领域模型的方法，通过结合结构性和语义启发式规则与迭代式LLM推理，克服上下文窗口限制，在十个项目的数据集上取得了高F1分数，适用于隐私敏感的工业环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的专有LLM因隐私和保密约束无法在工业场景中使用，而紧凑型开源LLM受限于上下文窗口无法直接处理大型代码库，因此需要一种能在本地部署且能处理大规模代码的领域模型提取方法。

Method: 结合结构性（代码结构）和语义（语义启发式）启发式方法，配合迭代式LLM推理。通过逐步分析排序后的代码元素子集来识别领域概念并优化领域边界，无需依赖完整的系统上下文。

Result: 在包含十个项目（每个项目涵盖人工策划的领域模型及其对应实现）的数据集上取得了高F1分数，且完全可在本地部署的LLM上执行。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持完全本地可部署的前提下实现高精度领域模型提取，特别适用于隐私敏感工业环境中的逆向工程任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities for code understanding, making them promising for reverse engineering domain models from source code. However, state-ofthe- art proprietary LLMs cannot be used in many industrial contexts due to privacy and confidentiality constraints, while compact open-source LLMs that can run locally are limited by their context window and cannot process large code bases directly. In this paper, we propose an automated approach to extract domain models from source code using lightweight, locally deployable LLMs. Our method combines structural and semantic heuristics with iterative LLM-based reasoning to overcome context limitations. By progressively analyzing ranked subsets of code elements, the approach identifies domain concepts and refines domain boundaries without requiring full-system context. Our approach achieves high F1-scores on a dataset of ten projects, each comprising a curated domain model and its corresponding implementation, while remaining fully executable on locally deployable LLMs. This makes it particularly suitable for reverse engineering tasks in privacy-sensitive industrial environments.

</details>


### [172] [The Role Specialization Model (RSM): Coordinating LLM-Based Tools in Agentic Software Development - An Exploratory Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12311)
*Carlos Alberto Fernández-y-Fernández,Jorge R. Aguilar-Cisneros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种角色分工模型（RSM），并通过案例研究协调三种基于LLM的工具（Antigravity、Gemini CLI、Qwen Code）协同完成一个Python桌面应用开发，评估其在ISO/IEC 25010质量模型下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正推动软件开发进入智能体软件工程（SE 3.0）范式，多个LLM工具在实际工作流中的角色协调问题亟待研究。当前缺乏对多工具角色分配与协同的实证探索。

Method: 提出角色专精模型（RSM），在一个交互式气候数据可视化Python桌面应用的增量开发中，按角色分配方式协调三种不同能力的LLM工具（云端IDE、CLI工具和本地模型），并记录工作流偏差、提示加固技术和定性质量评估。

Result: 明确的角色协调能够支持开发周期组织与架构质量，但需要精心的协调策略、上下文管理以及对智能体生成输出的人工验证。

Conclusion: RSM框架可作为SE 3.0范式下多LLM工具协同开发的有效组织方式，但人类监督与验证仍然不可或缺。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into software development workflows has given rise to a paradigm known as Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0), in which autonomous agents manage full development life cycles under human supervision. This paper presents an exploratory case study in which three LLM-based tools, Antigravity (an agentic IDE with a Gemini 2.5 backend), Gemini CLI, and Qwen Code (local execution via Ollama), are coordinated according to a role-distribution framework proposed in this work as the Role Specialization Model (RSM). Three research questions guide the study: (RQ1) how can LLM-based tools with distinct capabilities be coordinated through the RSM in a real development workflow; (RQ2) what deviations from the planned role distribution emerge during RSM execution and what factors explain them; and (RQ3) how does the resulting product compare against the ISO/IEC 25010 quality model. The objective was to propose the RSM with the incremental development of a Python desktop application for interactive climate-data visualization. The workflow, observed deviations, prompt-hardening techniques, and a qualitative quality assessment are documented. Results suggest that explicit role coordination can support development cycle organization and architectural quality, but requires deliberate coordination strategies, context management, and human verification of agent-generated outputs.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Self-evolving network verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11340)
*Ioannis Protogeros,Tibor Schneider,Laurent Vanbever*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出让网络验证器的符号模型自动演化，以忠实捕捉真实网络行为。通过反例引导循环，编码代理自动扩展验证器的符号编码，同时以路由器软件（如模拟路由器）作为可信Oracle提供真实路由状态。原型系统成功教会了一个3000行的SMT验证器三个原本不支持的协议特性，并能自主发现厂商特有行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号网络验证器需要专家手工编码协议和功能模型，但没有任何书面规范能完美描述网络实际行为：厂商实现偏离RFC，行为随版本变化。持续维护模型的负担使得许多需要验证的网络无法获得验证能力。

Method: 利用路由器软件本身作为唯一明确指定行为的来源，构建反例引导循环：编码代理提出对验证器符号编码的扩展，而可信Oracle（如模拟路由器）提供真实路由状态作为基准。代理通过每次与Oracle不一致的地方迭代细化网络模型，逐步自动演化出忠实反映实际行为的模型。

Result: 原型系统成功教会了3000行SMT验证器三个不支持的协议特性：OSPF区域、BGP路由反射、以及EVPN上的L3VPN，能够自主收敛到与Oracle匹配的模型，甚至发现厂商特有行为。

Conclusion: 自动化模型演化将核心难题从编写验证系统转向系统化测试验证系统。作者提出了一个关于信任和利用自动演化验证器的研究议程，为网络验证领域的自动化和可持续性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Symbolic network verifiers can reason about correctness across vast spaces of routing inputs and failures, but only for the protocols and features an expert has encoded by hand. Creating and maintaining a faithful model of the control plane is both difficult and never-ending, since no written source specifies perfectly what a network does: vendor implementations deviate from the RFCs, and behaviour shifts with releases. The burden of constant upkeep ultimately keeps verification out of many networks that need it.
  We argue that the model should instead evolve automatically to faithfully capture the actual network behaviour. To achieve that, we leverage the only source that specifies it unambiguously: the router software itself. In a counterexample-guided loop, a coding agent proposes extensions to the verifier's symbolic encoding, while a trusted oracle (e.g., emulated routers) supplies the ground-truth routing state. The agent iteratively refines the network model using each disagreement with the oracle.
  As early evidence, a prototype of this system taught a 3,000-line SMT-based verifier three features it did not support: OSPF areas, BGP route reflection, and L3VPN over EVPN, converging autonomously on models that match the oracle, even noticing vendor-specific behaviour. Automating model growth shifts the hard problem from writing verification systems to systematically testing them; we propose a research agenda for trusting and harnessing automatically evolved verifiers.

</details>


### [174] [Multi-Pair Fidelity-Aware Rate Allocation in a Quantum Network: Approximation Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11501)
*Zunzheng Zhang,Xuanli Lin,Zhaofeng Zhang,Nageswara S. V. Rao,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究量子网络中的多对保真度感知速率分配问题，形式化定义了三个速率分配问题（速率和、带最小速率约束的速率和、最大最小公平），证明这些问题均为NP困难，并提出在吞吐量或公平约束下最大化最小端到端保真度的优化版本，给出了完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中的纠缠分发必须同时考虑有限链路容量、概率性纠缠交换以及异构链路保真度，这些因素使得速率分配问题变得复杂。先前工作仅研究了所有链路具有相同保真度的特例，该特例存在多项式时间算法，但更一般的异构保真度场景尚未被研究。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了三个速率分配问题（速率和、带最小速率约束的速率和、最大最小公平），随后证明所有三个问题均为NP困难。接着研究这些问题的优化版本，即在吞吐量或公平性约束下最大化最小端到端保真度，并为这些优化问题提出了完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。

Result: 证明了所有三个速率分配问题均为NP困难，否定了该问题存在一般性多项式时间算法的可能性。同时成功提出了FPTAS来求解优化版本问题，并在随机生成的网络上进行了实验，验证了所提方案的计算有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了量子网络速率分配在异构保真度场景下的理论空白，虽然问题被证明是NP困难的，但通过FPTAS提供了可计算的有效近似方案，为量子网络中的资源分配提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Entanglement distribution in quantum networks must jointly account for limited link capacities, probabilistic entanglement swapping, and heterogeneous link fidelities. In this paper, we study multi-pair fidelity-aware rate allocation in quantum networks. We formulate three rate-allocation problems: rate sum, rate sum subject to minimum-rate constraints, and max-min fairness. Prior work has studied a special case of the rate sum problem, where all links have identical fidelity. This special case admits a polynomial-time algorithm. We prove that all three problems are NP-hard. We then study optimization versions of these problems which maximize the minimum end-to-end fidelity subject to throughput or fairness requirements. We present fully polynomial-time approximation schemes (FPTAS) for solving these optimization problems. Experiments on randomly generated networks demonstrate the computational effectiveness of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [175] [In QKD, Key Metadata is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11502)
*Alin-Bogdan Popa,Pantelimon George Popescu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文指出互联的QKD（量子密钥分发）网络已成为常态，但跨域密钥分发在语义层面仍有诸多未解决问题，提出通过QKD密钥元数据作为共同语言，将互操作性从语法层面提升到语义层面，并设计了6步标准化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多个QKD网络标准已就绪，技术上的互操作（语法层）已可行，但跨域密钥分发仍存在诸多未解决的问题：漏洞通告与缓解、空间段集成、QKD即服务的商业模型扩展、密钥新鲜度定制请求、管辖权、安全级别等。要实现从语法到语义的互操作，需要确保密钥传递的同时也传递其含义，因此需要一个共同语言。

Method: 提出通过QKD密钥元数据（QKD key metadata）作为承载语义的共同语言，并建议一个6步流程将其发展为标准，以实现在密钥传输的同时传递含义，从而达成语义互操作。

Result: 论文提出了一种概念性框架：利用密钥元数据作为语义互操作的基础语言，并规划了6步标准化流程，但尚未给出具体的元数据格式或标准实现细节。

Conclusion: 要实现QKD网络从语法互操作到语义互操作的跨越，必须开发QKD密钥元数据作为标准化的共同语言，通过6步流程来推进，以解决跨域密钥分发中的安全、商业和治理等综合问题。

Abstract: Federated interconnected QKD networks are becoming the norm, and with several standards already in place, interoperability is now technically feasible. Many aspects related to cross-domain key distribution, however, are left unresolved: breach announcement and mitigation, integration with the space segment, extension to a future QKD-as-a-Service commercial model, custom requests referring to key freshness, jurisdictions, security levels, etc. We argue that, to move from syntactic to semantic interoperability, a common language must be developed to ensure transfer of meaning along with the keys. We posit this is possible via QKD key metadata, and we propose a 6-step process to develop it as a standard.

</details>


### [176] [Integrated Sensing and Communication in 3GPP: Evolution from 5G-Advanced to 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11606)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了集成感知与通信（ISAC）在3GPP标准中从5G-Advanced到6G的演进，涵盖Release-19服务需求与信道模型、Release-20感知功能架构与报告抽象、5G单站基线用于无人机探测与跟踪，以及6G原生感知（被动目标感知、通信辅助、多感知模式、多源数据融合），旨在提供统一的技术视角审视标准化选择、实现权衡与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC将移动网络从信息传输扩展至被动目标和环境的感知，与传统雷达不同，它还需应对多应用接入、异构感知实体、不确定性、隐私、信任以及与通信服务的集成等挑战。作者希望通过梳理3GPP标准演进，提供一个统一的技术视角，帮助理解当前标准化选择及其实现权衡，并指出网络级感知面临的开放挑战。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性文章，系统回顾了ISAC在3GPP中的演进过程：详细解读Release-19的ISAC服务需求与信道模型，Release-20的感知功能架构与报告抽象，以及5G单站基线在无人机检测与跟踪中的应用；同时探讨了6G原生感知的多个方面，包括被动目标感知、通信辅助、多种感知模式和多源数据集成。

Result: 文章连接了5G-Advanced与6G之间不断演进的3GPP架构和无线电研究，提供了一个关于当前标准化选择的统一技术观点，揭示了实现中的权衡以及塑造网络级感知的开放挑战。

Conclusion: ISAC在移动网络中的应用正从5G-Advanced逐步演进至6G原生感知，其核心价值在于复用授权频谱、基础设施和边缘计算，在网络规模下实现受运营商控制的感知能力，但仍面临隐私、信任、多模式融合等开放挑战。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) extends mobile networks from information transfer toward perception of passive objects and environments. By exploiting propagation delay, Doppler, angle, and temporal variation, a mobile network can support detection and tracking while reusing licensed spectrum, infrastructure, and edge computing at network scale and under operator control. Unlike conventional radar, ISAC must additionally address multi-application access, heterogeneous sensing entities, uncertainty, privacy, trust, and integration with communication services. This article reviews the evolution of ISAC in 3GPP from 5G-Advanced to 6G. It explains Release-19 ISAC service requirements and channel models, the Release-20 sensing function architecture and reporting abstractions, and the 5G monostatic baseline for drone detection and tracking. It also examines 6G-native sensing, including passive object sensing, communication assistance, multiple sensing modes, and multi-source data integration. By connecting the evolving 3GPP architecture and radio studies across 5G-Advanced and 6G, this article provides a unified technical perspective on current standardization choices, their implementation tradeoffs, and the open challenges shaping network-grade sensing.

</details>


### [177] [On the Allocation of Transmit Power for Coordinated Spatial Reuse in IEEE 802.11bn Multi-Access Point Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11971)
*Francesc Wilhelmi,Boris Bellalta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对IEEE 802.11bn协调空间复用（Co-SR）的比例公平（PF）驱动功率分配框架。证明任何帕累托最优功率对至少有一个AP保持最大功率，将二维搜索降为一维线搜索，并在离散速率系统中得到精确闭式解。通过暴力最优对照和Kom8ndor模拟验证，发现联合评估两个AP链路是释放空间复用增益的关键，且当前11bn信令仅支持自私策略而非公平最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEEE 802.11bn引入了Co-SR机制，允许两个AP协调控制发射功率进行同时传输，但如何在每次传输机会（TXOP）基础上合理分配Co-SR发射功率以兼顾比例公平性，尚未有系统的解决框架，且需要控制在TXOP时限内的低复杂度方案。

Method: 提出PF驱动的Co-SR发射功率分配框架：1) 证明帕累托最优功率对中至少一个AP保持最大功率，从而将二维搜索降为两条一维线搜索；2) 在真实离散速率（MCS）系统中推导出精确闭式解；3) 与暴力最优对照及低复杂度自私基线策略进行对比验证，并通过Kom8ndor 11bn网络模拟器进行包级仿真。

Result: 联合评估两个AP的链路是释放Co-SR空间复用增益的关键；协调AP的公平最优功率在连续速率模型与真实离散MCS模型下结果差异显著；当前11bn信令支持自私策略，但公平最优策略需要额外的按TXOP信道上报。

Conclusion: 所提出的PF驱动框架能够高效地在TXOP时间预算内求解公平最优的Co-SR功率分配，但现有11bn标准信令不足以支撑最优公平策略，未来需要扩展信令以支持按TXOP的信道信息上报。

Abstract: IEEE 802.11bn (11bn) introduces Coordinated Spatial Reuse (Co-SR), a Multi-AP Coordination (MAPC) scheme in which two Access Points (APs) coordinate to control the transmit power for a simultaneous transmission. This letter introduces a Proportional Fairness (PF)-driven framework for allocating Co-SR transmit power on a per-Transmission Opportunity (TXOP) basis. We prove that any Pareto-optimal power pair keeps at least one AP at its maximum power, reducing the joint two-dimensional search to two cheap one-dimensional line searches that fit comfortably within TXOP timing, and show that this collapses to an exact closed-form expression in a real, discrete-rate system. We validate the resulting policies, together with a low-complexity selfish baseline, against a brute-force oracle and through packet-level simulations in Kom8ndor, an 11bn network simulator. Results show that jointly evaluating both APs' links is key to unlocking Co-SR's spatial reuse gains, that the coordinated AP's fairness-optimal power depends critically on whether the rate is modeled continuously or through real, discrete Modulation and Coding Scheme (MCS) steps, and that current 11bn signaling supports the selfish policy but not the fairness-optimal ones, which would need additional per-TXOP channel reporting.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究两个目标对立的LLM代理在多轮交互中因缺乏共享目标函数而导致的"协作崩溃"问题，提出Experience Orchestrator (EO)——一个基于控制理论的治理层——在模拟金融服务环境中通过Contextual Bandit、PID控制器和POMDP信念追踪三个机制来引导联合轨迹。60,000次模拟显示EO将高意向顾问接触率从46.1%提升至78.1%（+32个百分点），其中CB变体选择解释了97%的组间结果方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个LLM代理具有结构性对立目标时，缺少共享目标函数会导致对话崩溃而非竞争：访客妥协、站点代理停止变换策略、对话在没有达成任何一方目标的情况下终止。论文想要探究能否用控制理论驱动的治理层来替代缺失的目标函数。

Method: 提出Experience Orchestrator (EO)治理层，包含三个机制：(1) Contextual Bandit (CB)根据真实网络分析数据校准的内容臂选择器；(2) PID控制器通过动态模式约束强制执行行为一致性；(3) POMDP信念追踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。在模拟金融服务环境中，站点代理引导访客联系顾问，同时访客保持心理学上真实的抵抗行为，进行60,000次模拟实验。

Result: EO实现了高意向顾问接触率78.1%对比朴素LLM对照组46.1%，提升32个百分点。CB变体选择解释了97%的组间结果方差，表明治理策略而非环境初始条件决定最终轨迹。个体层面分析揭示两种截然不同的模式：对无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是系统功能与非功能的分水岭；对已接近对齐的访客，朴素LLM的共情默认策略基本足够。

Conclusion: 治理层确实能替代缺失的目标函数，但其有效性高度依赖访客的初始状态。所有发现都基于LLM到LLM的模拟，PID控制器未经真实人类不可预测性校准，在真实流量上验证EO是关键的下一步。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance.
